Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in transcription theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 967 and the invariant I(A,B) = 771 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 967 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the transcription structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 771. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
